Let's write final asm:

```
__asm__ __volatile__(
    "pushw %6\n\t"
    "pushw %5\n\t"
    "pushw %4\n\t"
    "pushw %3\n\t"
    "pushw %2\n\t"
    "pushw %1\n\t"
    "pushw %0\n\t"
    "pushw %%bp\n\t"
    "movw %%sp, %%bp\n\t"
    "subw $2, %%sp\n\t"
    "movw $0, -2(%%bp)\n\t"

    /* zero s[0..m-1] */
    "movw 8(%%bp), %%si\n\t"
    "movw 2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 5f\n\t"
    "1:\n\t"
    "fldz\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%si)\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%si\n\t"
    "decw %%cx\n\t"
    "jnz 1b\n\t"
    "5:\n\t"

    /* outer loop */
    "2:\n\t"
    "movw -2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "cmpw 4(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "jge 4f\n\t"
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%dx\n\t"   /* q[i] */
    "fldz\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%dx)\n\t"
    "movw 6(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"    /* Arow */
    "movw 8(%%bp), %%si\n\t"    /* s */
    "movw 12(%%bp), %%di\n\t"   /* p */
    "movw 2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"    /* m */
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%dx\n\t"   /* q_cur */
    "movw 14(%%bp), %%ax\n\t"   /* r_cur */
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 6f\n\t"
    "3:\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fmull (%%ax)\n\t"
    "faddl (%%si)\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%si)\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fmull (%%di)\n\t"
    "faddl (%%dx)\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%dx)\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%bx\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%si\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%di\n\t"
    "decw %%cx\n\t"
    "jnz 3b\n\t"
    "6:\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%dx\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%ax\n\t"
    "movw %%dx, 10(%%bp)\n\t"
    "movw %%ax, 14(%%bp)\n\t"
    "movw %%bx, 6(%%bp)\n\t"
    "incw -2(%%bp)\n\t"
    "jmp 2b\n\t"
    "4:\n\t"

    "movw %%bp, %%sp\n\t"
    "popw %%bp\n\t"
    "addw $14, %%sp\n\t"
    : /* no outputs */
    : "rm"(m), "rm"(n), "rm"(A), "rm"(s), "rm"(q), "rm"(p), "rm"(r)
    : "ax", "bx", "cx", "dx", "si", "di", "memory"
);
```

Need verify stack frame offsets after pushing inputs and old bp. We pushed in order: r, p, q, s, A, n, m, old bp. Then bp points to old bp. Recall stack grows downward; after push r, then p, ... Let's compute:
- Start sp S.
- push r: sp S-2, [S-2]=r
- push p: sp S-4, [S-4]=p
- push q: sp S-6, [S-6]=q
- push s: sp S-8, [S-8]=s
- push A: sp S-10, [S-10]=A
- push n: sp S-12, [S-12]=n
- push m: sp S-14, [S-14]=m
- push bp: sp S-16, [S-16]=old bp
- mov sp,bp: bp = S-16.
Then offsets:
2(%bp) = [S-14] = m. yes.
4 = n? [S-12] = n. yes.
6 = A? [S-10] = A. yes.
8 = s? [S-8] = s. yes.
10 = q? [S-6] = q. yes.
12 = p? [S-4] = p. yes.
14 = r? [S-2] = r. yes.
Good.
At end `mov bp,sp` sp=S-16; `pop bp`; sp=S-14; bp restored; `add $14,sp`; sp=S. Good.

Let's review final asm in full with C syntax.

```
__asm__ __volatile__(
    "pushw %6\n\t"
    "pushw %5\n\t"
    "pushw %4\n\t"
    "pushw %3\n\t"
    "pushw %2\n\t"
    "pushw %1\n\t"
    "pushw %0\n\t"
    "pushw %%bp\n\t"
    "movw %%sp, %%bp\n\t"
    "subw $2, %%sp\n\t"
    "movw $0, -2(%%bp)\n\t"
    "movw 8(%%bp), %%si\n\t"
    "movw 2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 5f\n\t"
    "1:\n\t"
    "fldz\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%si)\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%si\n\t"
    "decw %%cx\n\t"
    "jnz 1b\n\t"
    "5:\n\t"
    "2:\n\t"
    "movw -2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "cmpw 4(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "jge 4f\n\t"
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fldz\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "movw 14(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "movw 6(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "movw 8(%%bp), %%si\n\t"
    "movw 12(%%bp), %%di\n\t"
    "movw 2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 6f\n\t"
    "3:\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fmul %%st(2)\n\t"
    "faddl (%%si)\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%si)\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fmull (%%di)\n\t"
    "faddp\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%bx\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%si\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%di\n\t"
    "decw %%cx\n\t"
    "jnz 3b\n\t"
    "6:\n\t"
    "movw %%bx, 6(%%bp)\n\t"
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fstp %%st(0)\n\t"
    "addw $8, 10(%%bp)\n\t"
    "addw $8, 14(%%bp)\n\t"
    "incw -2(%%bp)\n\t"
    "jmp 2b\n\t"
    "4:\n\t"
    "movw %%bp, %%sp\n\t"
    "popw %%bp\n\t"
    "addw $14, %%sp\n\t"
    : /* no outputs */
    : "rm"(m), "rm"(n), "rm"(A), "rm"(s), "rm"(q), "rm"(p), "rm"(r)
    : "bx", "cx", "si", "di", "memory", "cc"
);
```

Full final code:

```
__asm__ __volatile__(
    "pushw %6\n\t"
    "pushw %5\n\t"
    "pushw %4\n\t"
    "pushw %3\n\t"
    "pushw %2\n\t"
    "pushw %1\n\t"
    "pushw %0\n\t"
    "pushw %%bp\n\t"
    "movw %%sp, %%bp\n\t"
    "subw $2, %%sp\n\t"
    "movw $0, -2(%%bp)\n\t"
    "movw 8(%%bp), %%si\n\t"
    "movw 2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 5f\n\t"
    "1:\n\t"
    "fldz\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%si)\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%si\n\t"
    "decw %%cx\n\t"
    "jnz 1b\n\t"
    "5:\n\t"
    "2:\n\t"
    "movw -2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "cmpw 4(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "jge 4f\n\t"
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fldz\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "movw 14(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "movw 6(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "movw 8(%%bp), %%si\n\t"
    "movw 12(%%bp), %%di\n\t"
    "movw 2(%%bp), %%cx\n\t"
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 6f\n\t"
    "3:\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fmul %%st(2)\n\t"
    "faddl (%%si)\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%si)\n\t"
    "fldl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fmull (%%di)\n\t"
    "faddp\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%bx\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%si\n\t"
    "addw $8, %%di\n\t"
    "decw %%cx\n\t"
    "jnz 3b\n\t"
    "6:\n\t"
    "movw %%bx, 6(%%bp)\n\t"
    "movw 10(%%bp), %%bx\n\t"
    "fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "fstp %%st(0)\n\t"
    "addw $8, 10(%%bp)\n\t"
    "addw $8, 14(%%bp)\n\t"
    "incw -2(%%bp)\n\t"
    "jmp 2b\n\t"
    "4:\n\t"
    "movw %%bp, %%sp\n\t"
    "popw %%bp\n\t"
    "addw $14, %%sp\n\t"
    :
    : "rm"(m), "rm"(n), "rm"(A), "rm"(s), "rm"(q), "rm"(p), "rm"(r)
    : "bx", "cx", "si", "di", "memory", "cc"
);
```
